[Director 指令] **[紧急] GPU0 崩溃复发 + GPU1 速度异常，立即处理**

Reviewer 报告 plan-001b-gpu0 第二次崩溃，GPU0 已空转 ~8 小时。GPU1 速度异常慢（9.5h 仅 2 episodes，预期 4 min/episode）。按以下顺序处理：

**A. GPU0（优先级最高）**
1. `kill -9 794005`（孤儿 vLLM 进程，占 28GB 显存）
2. 排查 vLLM init 反复失败的根因——不是简单的"残留进程占显存"，上次杀了残留重提交后又在 init 阶段崩溃。诊断步骤：
   - 检查 `llm_agent.py:196` 的 LLM() 构造参数（device / tensor_parallel_size / gpu_memory_utilization）
   - 在 GPU0 上手动隔离测试：`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python -c "from vllm import LLM; LLM('Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct')"`
   - 检查 GPU0 硬件状态：`nvidia-smi -i 0`，看 ECC errors / temperature
   - 检查完整 crash log 找具体失败原因
3. 修复后重新提交 plan-001b-gpu0
4. 更新 registry plan-001b-gpu0 status 为 FAILED（当前显示 RUNNING 已过时）

**B. GPU1 速度异常**
- 当前：~4.75h/episode，预期 ~4min/episode，慢了 ~70 倍
- 检查：ScienceWorld env reset 耗时？Java 进程卡顿？vLLM 推理延迟？每步 LLM 调用耗时日志？
- 如果是 env 瓶颈，考虑在 episodes 之间加 env 进程回收

先 A 后 B，A 完成前不要做其他任务。回报诊断结果和修复方案。

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断 GPU1 plan-001b-gpu1 速度异常\n\n### 背景\nplan-001b-gpu1 运行 ~9.5h 仅完成 ~2 episodes，预期 4 min/episode，慢了 ~70 倍。\n\n### 诊断步骤（全部用 ssh_read_only）\n\n1. 检查当前进度：\n   ```\n   tail -100 ~/runs/simgate-modular-routing/plan-001b-gpu1.log\n   ```\n   看实际完成了多少 episodes，当前在做什么\n\n2. 检查 plan_c 输出目录已有多少数据：\n   ```\n   for task in find-non-living-thing grow-fruit inclined-plane-determine-angle identify-life-stages-2; do\n     echo \"=== $task ===\"\n     wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/plan_c/$task/episodes.jsonl 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_FILE\"\n   done\n   ```\n\n3. 检查每步 LLM 调用耗时——搜索日志中的时间戳模式：\n   ```\n   grep -E \"(episode|step|latency|time|elapsed|toks/s)\" ~/runs/simgate-modular-routing/plan-001b-gpu1.log | tail -50\n   ```\n\n4. 检查 ScienceWorld Java 进程状态：\n   ```\n   ps aux | grep -i java | grep -v grep\n   ```\n   看 Java 进程 CPU/内存使用是否异常\n\n5. 检查系统资源：\n   ```\n   top -bn1 | head -20\n   free -h\n   df -h /root/autodl-tmp/\n   ```\n\n6. 检查 GPU1 利用率和显存：\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi -i 1\n   ```\n\n7. 如果有 per-step 日志，分析瓶颈在哪里：\n   - LLM 推理（vLLM tok/s）\n   - ScienceWorld env.step()（Java 侧）\n   - Python 处理逻辑\n   - 磁盘 IO\n\n### 产出\n- 诊断报告：实际完成 episodes 数、每 episode 耗时、瓶颈位置\n- 建议修复方案（如果有）\n- 如果是 ScienceWorld env 瓶颈，建议在 episodes 之间回收 env 进程\n\n### 服务器\nwestb-13313, account root。只读诊断，不要 kill 或修改 GPU1 上的任何进程。",

```text
Great results from the plan-012 dry-run. Key findings:

1. **Aggregate results** (30 tasks, 249 episodes): Δ_full=+38.33, Δ_nd=+0.48, death_fraction=98.75%, r=0.986
2. **Outlier update**: measure-melting-point-unknown DAF=59.4% (not 0.38 as previously noted)
3. **Genuine-det tasks** with DAF < 95%: only 4, not 5:
   - measure-melting-point-unknown: 59.4%, Δ_nd=+16.5
   - find-living-thing: 89.2%, Δ_nd=+4.5
   - find-plant: 93.5%, Δ_nd=+4.0
   - find-non-living-thing: -0.0%, Δ_nd=-6.8 (internal actually better)
4. **Paper assets**: 3 figures + table data all generated and ready

Wait, I need to reconcile this with the earlier count—we had identified 5 genuine-det tasks including find-animal and identify-life-stages-1, so something's off with the current list.

Looking at the discrepancy, the prescreening data now covers 30 total tasks instead of what was in the original proposal, and I'm using different criteria here: the genuine-det definition relied on Δ_nd > +3 to show non-death advantage, while the DAF < 95% threshold is a separate metric, so these sets won't necessarily align. Rather than dig deeper into this now, I need to focus on the GPU0 fix the Director prioritized—that's blocking everything else. I'll register the artifacts but hold off on reporting until the gpu0-fix worker completes.

```text
Excellent! The GPU0 fix is complete:

**Root cause**: vLLM EngineCore child process becomes orphan when parent script crashes, holding ~28 GiB GPU memory. Re-submission hits OOM because orphan still holds memory. No cleanup mechanism existed.

**Fix**: Added `cleanup_gpu_processes()` function to `episode_level_collection.py` (lines 36-60) that auto-kills residual processes on target GPU before agent creation.

**Current state**:
- GPU0: plan-001b-gpu0-v2 (PID 853847) running normally, 100% utilization


- GPU1: plan-001b-gpu1 (PID 774317) running normally

Now I need to update the registry to mark the old plan-001b-gpu0 as failed and register the new v2 version, report the diagnosis to Director, then spawn a worker to investigate the GPU1 speed anomaly.
```

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断 GPU1 plan-001b 速度异常\n\n### 问题\nplan-001b-gpu1 (PID 774317) 运行 ~9.5h 但仅完成 ~2 episodes。预期 4 min/episode，实际 ~4.75h/episode，慢了约 70 倍。\n\n### 诊断步骤（全部用 ssh_read_only）\n\n1. 检查当前进度：\n   ```\n   # 输出目录里有多少 episode 数据\n   for task in find-non-living-thing grow-fruit inclined-plane-determine-angle identify-life-stages-2; do\n     echo \"=== $task ===\"\n     wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/plan_c/$task/episodes.jsonl 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_FILE\"\n     ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/plan_c/$task/ 2>/dev/null | tail -5\n   done\n   ```\n\n2. 检查运行日志，找速度瓶颈：\n   ```\n   tail -100 ~/runs/simgate-modular-routing/plan-001b-gpu1.log\n   ```\n\n3. 分析每步耗时：\n   ```\n   # 看最近的 step 时间戳间隔\n   grep -E \"step|episode|Episode|Step|action|Action\" ~/runs/simgate-modular-routing/plan-001b-gpu1.log | tail -30\n   ```\n\n4. 检查 ScienceWorld Java 进程：\n   ```\n   ps aux | grep -i \"java\\|scienceworld\" | grep -v grep\n   # Java 进程内存和 CPU 使用\n   top -b -n1 | grep java | head -5\n   ```\n\n5. 检查系统资源：\n   ```\n   # CPU/内存瓶颈？\n   top -b -n1 | head -20\n   free -h\n   # disk I/O?\n   iostat -x 1 3 2>/dev/null || df -h\n   ```\n\n6. 检查 vLLM 推理延迟：\n   ```\n   # 看日志里的 toks/s 或 latency 信息\n   grep -iE \"toks|token|latency|time|speed\" ~/runs/simgate-modular-routing/plan-001b-gpu1.log | tail -20\n   ```\n\n7. 对比 prescreening 的速度（prescreening 是正常速度）：\n   ```\n   # prescreening GPU1 日志\n   tail -20 /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/prescreening/gpu1_run.log 2>/dev/null\n   ```\n\n8. 检查是否有 ScienceWorld env reset 卡顿：\n   ```\n   # 看是否有长时间的 gap\n   grep -iE \"reset|init|start.*episode|end.*episode\" ~/runs/simgate-modular-routing/plan-001b-gpu1.log | tail -20\n   ```\n\n### 产出\n- 速度瓶颈根因（ScienceWorld? vLLM? I/O? 内存?）\n- 每步平均耗时 breakdown\n- 修复建议\n\n### 服务器\nwestb-13313, account root",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ],
  "context": "plan-001b-gpu1 速度异常：预期 4 min/episode，实际 ~4.75h/episode。prescreening 阶段速度正常。两者用同一代码和模型，唯一区别是 plan-001b 加了 --reuse_dirs 和输出到不同目录。"

SSH 到 westb-13313（ssh_read_only），检查两个文件的状态：\n   ```\n   # 被覆盖的文件\n   wc -c /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/death_decomposition_report.json\n   python3 -c \"import json; d=json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/death_decomposition_report.json')); print(f'tasks: {len(d.get(\\\"per_task\\\", d.get(\\\"tasks\\\", [])))}') if isinstance(d, dict) else print(d)\" 2>/dev/null\n   \n   # dry-run 完整版\n   wc -c /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/decomposition_dryrun/death_decomposition_report.json\n   python3 -c \"import json; d=json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/decomposition_dryrun/death_decomposition_report.json')); print(f'tasks: {len(d.get(\\\"per_task\\\", d.get(\\\"tasks\\\", [])))}') if isinstance(d, dict) else print(d)\" 2>/dev/null\n   ```\n\n2. 如果 decomposition_dryrun 版本是完整的（30 tasks），用 ssh_execute 复制：\n   ```\n   cp /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/decomposition_dryrun/death_decomposition_report.json /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/death_decomposition_report.json\n   ```\n\n3. 如果 decomposition_dryrun 版本也不完整，重新生成：\n   ```\n   cd /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing\n   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n   python src/death_decomposition.py --data_dirs results/prescreening,results/mvp,results/research --output_dir results/\n   ```\n\n4. 验证恢复后的文件：\n   ```\n   python3 -c \"\n   import json\n   d = json.load(open('/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/death_decomposition_report.json'))\n   tasks = d.get('per_task', d.get('tasks', []))\n   print(f'Tasks: {len(tasks)}')\n   agg = d.get('aggregate', {})\n   print(f'Δ_full: {agg.get(\\\"delta_full\\\", \\\"N/A\\\")}')\n   print(f'r: {agg.get(\\\"correlation_r\\\", \\\"N/A\\\")}')\n   \"\n   ```\n\n### 注意\n- 不要碰正在运行的 plan-001b 进程（gpu0 PID 853847, gpu1 PID 774317）\n- 只操作 results/ 目录下的 JSON 文件\n\n### 服务器\nwestb-13313, account root",

}

{
  "project": "simgate-modular-routing",
  "file": "memory.md",
  "content": "# Memory — simgate-modular-routing\n\n## 决策记录\n\n### plan-011 已关闭 (2026-05-13)\nDirector 决策：接受 keywords v1 (always-det) + v2 (always-internal) 结果，不设计第三种 heuristic。\n定位为论文 \"structural failure analysis\"——keyword routing 在 ScienceWorld episode-level 结构性失效。\n\n### plan-001b blocking gate (2026-05-13)\n4 项检查全部通过：pipeline-format-fix ✅, dry-run ✅, episodes.jsonl ✅, classify_episode_labels() 三标签 ✅\n\n### D013 framing pivot (2026-05-13)\n核心方向：death avoidance dominates routing signal (~99%)。\n论文贡献 = empirical finding + counterfactual methodology，非 router 架构。\n实测数据：Δ_full=+38.33, Δ_nd=+0.48, r=0.986 p<0.0001 (30 tasks, 249 episodes)。\n\n### 预筛最终结果 (2026-05-13, 25/25 tasks)\n- death-dominated: 19, genuine-det: 5, internal-preferred: 1\n- Genuine-det (DAF < 95%): measure-melting-point-unknown (59.4%), find-living-thing (89.2%), find-plant (93.5%), find-non-living-thing (0%, int-preferred)\n- 注意：DAF 分类与原始 Δ_nd > +3 分类有差异，plan-001b 完整数据后需重新评估\n\n## 经验教训\n\n### vLLM EngineCore 孤儿进程 (2026-05-13)\n**关键**：vLLM 子进程（EngineCore）在父脚本崩溃后成为孤儿，持续占用 GPU 显存。必须在脚本启动时清理目标 GPU 的残留进程。已在 episode_level_collection.py 添加 cleanup_gpu_processes()。\n\n### Episode 收集速度基准 (2026-05-13)\n每 episode ~4.7 min：always-internal ~31s + always-deterministic ~200s（每步 3×JVM fork+replay history）。\n加速选项：减少 n_candidates、环境池化、增量 replay（需 ScienceWorld 支持 save/load）。\n\n### plan-004 ablation 代码就绪 (2026-05-13)\n4 个文件已部署：ablation_eval_common.py, ablation_data_converter.py, ablation_implicit_1_7b.py, ablation_implicit_7b.py。\n模型 ID: Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B (condition a/b), Qwen/Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (condition c)。\n"
}

_source line 479 · 2026-05-13T22:50:56.316Z_

[Director 指令] Reviewer 审查了 router_sft_train.py，发现三个必须在 plan-002 训练前修复的问题。plan-001b 在跑不要动，利用等待时间修复：

**1. 数据泄漏修复（最重要）**
`stratified_split()` 当前按 step-level random split，但同一 episode 的 ~30 个 step 共享同一 label 且 observation 高度相关。改为 **episode-level split**：先按 episode 分 train/val，再展开为 step-level samples。保留 stratified 逻辑但分层单位从 step 改为 episode。

**2. Best model 指标修复**
`metric_for_best_model="accuracy"` 在 102 det vs 1 internal 的极端不平衡下无意义（always-det = 99%）。改为 `"f1_macro"` 或 `"balanced_accuracy"`。`compute_metrics()` 已算了 per-class F1，直接用。

**3. 清理过时代码**
删除 `postprocess_to_sft.py`（146行，硬编码旧路径 + 旧格式，与当前 pipeline 不兼容）。

**4. 提前下载 Qwen3-1.7B**
服务器上还没有这个模型。趁等待时间用 ModelScope 下载到 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/`：
```
source /etc/network_turbo
python -c "from modelscope import snapshot_download; snapshot_download('Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/models')"
```

以上 4 项可以并行 Worker 处理。完成后汇报。

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：修复 router_sft_train.py 三个问题 + 清理过时代码\n\n### 背景\nReviewer 审查了 router_sft_train.py，发现 3 个必须在 plan-002 训练前修复的问题。plan-001b 数据收集在跑（不要碰），利用等待时间修复代码。\n\n### 修复 1：数据泄漏 — Episode-level split（最重要）\n\n当前 `stratified_split()` 按 step-level random split，但同一 episode 的 ~30 个 step 共享同一 label 且 observation 高度相关，导致数据泄漏。\n\n**修改方案**：\n1. 找到 `stratified_split()` 函数\n2. 改为 episode-level split：\n   - 从 sft_triples.jsonl 读取时，每条记录应有 episode 标识（task_type + episode_idx）\n   - 先按 episode 分组\n   - 按 episode 的 label（episode-level label，不是 step-level）做 stratified split\n   - 分完后展开为 step-level samples\n3. 保留 stratified 逻辑但分层单位从 step 改为 episode\n4. 如果 sft_triples.jsonl 没有 episode_idx 字段，需要用 task_type 做 leave-one-task-out split（已有 --eval_strategy 参数）\n\n### 修复 2：Best model 指标\n\n当前 `metric_for_best_model=\"accuracy\"` 在极端不平衡下（102 det vs 1 internal）无意义。\n\n**修改**：\n- 改为 `metric_for_best_model=\"f1_macro\"` 或 `\"balanced_accuracy\"`\n- `compute_metrics()` 应已计算 per-class F1，确认可直接引用\n- 同时更新 `greater_is_better=True`\n\n### 修复 3：清理过时代码\n\n删除 `src/postprocess_to_sft.py`（146行，硬编码旧路径 + 旧格式，与当前 pipeline 不兼容）。\n\n### 验证\n\n修复完成后：\n1. `python src/router_sft_train.py --help` 确认参数正常\n2. 用现有的 sft_triples.jsonl 做 dry-run：`python src/router_sft_train.py --data_path results/sft_data_death_aware/sft_triples.jsonl --dry_run` （或 --max_steps 1）\n3. 确认 episode-level split 逻辑在 dry-run 中正确执行（日志应显示 \"N episodes in train, M episodes in val\"）\n\n### 服务器\n- westb-13313, account root\n- 项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing\n- Python 环境：source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base",

SSH 到 westb-13313（ssh_execute），使用 ModelScope 下载（AutoDL 代理下 HuggingFace 很慢，ModelScope 国内 CDN 快得多）：\n   ```\n   source /etc/network_turbo\n   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n   pip install modelscope -q 2>/dev/null\n   python -c \"from modelscope import snapshot_download; snapshot_download('Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/models')\"\n   ```\n\n2. 如果 ModelScope 没有 Qwen3-1.7B（可能 ID 不同），尝试：\n   ```\n   python -c \"from modelscope import snapshot_download; snapshot_download('qwen/Qwen3-1.7B', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/models')\"\n   ```\n   或用 HuggingFace（加代理）：\n   ```\n   export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\n   export HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\n   export REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n   export SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n   python -c \"from huggingface_hub import snapshot_download; snapshot_download('Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/models')\"\n   ```\n\n3. 下载完成后验证：\n   ```\n   ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B/ 2>/dev/null || ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/models/qwen/Qwen3-1.7B/\n   python -c \"\n   from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\n   import os\n   model_path = '/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B'\n   if not os.path.exists(model_path):\n       model_path = '/root/autodl-tmp/models/qwen/Qwen3-1.7B'\n   tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n   print(f'Tokenizer loaded: vocab_size={tok.vocab_size}')\n   print('Model files present:', os.listdir(model_path)[:10])\n   \"\n   ```\n\n4. 报告模型本地路径和文件大小\n\n### 注意\n- 不要碰正在运行的 plan-001b 进程\n- 下载可能需要几分钟，ModelScope CDN 通常较快\n- 如果 modelscope 报错，尝试 `pip install modelscope --upgrade`\n\n### 服务器\nwestb-13313, account root",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：编写 plan-005 Routing Pattern Analysis 脚本\n\n### 背景\nplan-005 分析 routing 模式，输出论文 Figure（routing heatmap + failure mode taxonomy）。不依赖 plan-003 trained router，直接从 plan-001b counterfactual episode 数据分析。\n\n### 你要做的\n\n1. SSH 到 westb-13313，在 `/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/src/` 创建 `routing_pattern_analysis.py`\n\n2. 功能模块：\n\n   **A. Per-task routing metrics extraction**\n   - 输入：`results/plan_c/` (或任意 data_dirs) 下的 episodes.jsonl\n   - 每个 episode 含 always_internal 和 always_deterministic 两个 run\n   - 提取每个 task 的：\n     - det-preferred rate（det score > int score 的 episode 占比）\n     - internal death rate vs det death rate（score = -100）\n     - score variance (internal vs det)\n     - death avoidance fraction (DAF)\n     - mean Δ(D-I)、Δ_nd (non-death only)\n   \n   **B. Routing heatmap figure**\n   - task × metric 矩阵图（matplotlib）\n   - X轴：metrics (death_rate_int, death_rate_det, DAF, Δ_full, Δ_nd, det_preferred_rate)\n   - Y轴：tasks（按 DAF 排序）\n   - 颜色：归一化到 [0,1] 或用 diverging colormap\n   - 标注 genuine-det tasks（DAF < 95%）\n   - 输出 PDF + PNG\n   \n   **C. Failure mode taxonomy**\n   - 从 deterministic-routing episodes 中提取 step-level observations\n   - 聚类分析：按 observation 文本特征（关键词 TF-IDF + 简单聚类）\n   - 识别常见 failure modes：\n     - death-related（observation 包含死亡/惩罚信号）\n     - navigation failures（重复动作、循环）\n     - reasoning errors（错误的物品/操作选择）\n   - 输出：failure mode 表格 + 每类 top-3 example observations\n   \n   **D. CLI interface**\n   ```\n   python src/routing_pattern_analysis.py \\\n     --data_dirs results/plan_c,results/prescreening,results/mvp \\\n     --output_dir results/routing_analysis/ \\\n     --min_episodes 5\n   ```\n\n3. 用现有 prescreening 数据做 dry-run 验证（至少生成 heatmap 预览）\n\n4. 学术论文级图表样式：\n   - 清晰字体（serif 或 sans-serif, 10-12pt）\n   - 合适尺寸（单栏 ~3.5in 或双栏 ~7in）\n   - 无多余装饰（no gridlines, minimal borders）\n   - colorbar 有标签\n\n### 服务器\nwestb-13313, account root\nPython: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n已安装：matplotlib, numpy, scikit-learn（如需其他包可 pip install）",

{
  "task_type": "code:plan-005-routing-pattern",
  "project": "simgate-modular-routing",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：创建 plan-005 routing_pattern_analysis.py\n\n### 背景\nplan-005 分析 per-task routing 模式，直接从 plan-001b episode 数据中提取（不依赖 plan-003 trained router）。输出论文 Figure 和 failure mode taxonomy。\n\n### 你要创建的文件：`src/routing_pattern_analysis.py`\n\n**功能 1：Per-task routing 模式提取**\n- 输入：`--data_dirs results/prescreening,results/mvp,results/research,results/plan_c`\n- 对每个 task 计算：\n  - det-preferred rate（episode 中 det > internal 的比例）\n  - death rate（internal / deterministic 各自的 death rate）\n  - score variance（internal / deterministic 各自的 score 标准差）\n  - mean score gap（Δ(D-I)）\n  - death avoidance fraction (DAF)\n- 输出 JSON summary per task\n\n**功能 2：Routing heatmap figure**\n- task × metric 矩阵图（matplotlib）\n- 行 = task types（按 signal category 分组：genuine-det, death-dominated, internal-preferred）\n- 列 = metrics（det_pref_rate, int_death_rate, det_death_rate, Δ(D-I), DAF）\n- 颜色编码，标注数值\n- 输出 PDF + PNG\n\n**功能 3：Failure mode taxonomy**\n- 从 deterministic-preferred episodes 中提取 step-level 特征：\n  - 哪些 step 触发 internal death（action 类型、observation 关键词）\n  - death step 的 observation 中常见的模式（e.g., \"you have died\", score=-100 触发的 action）\n- 聚类分析（简单的关键词频率统计即可，不需要复杂的 embedding clustering）\n- 输出 failure mode 列表 + 频率\n\n**参数**：\n- `--data_dirs`: 逗号分隔的数据目录（与 death_decomposition.py 一致）\n- `--output_dir`: 输出目录\n- `--min_episodes`: 最少 episode 数（默认 5，过滤不足的 task）\n\n**数据格式**：\n- episodes.jsonl 每行：`{episode_idx, variation_idx, always_internal: {final_score, steps: [{step, action, modality, observation, score_before, score_after}]}, always_deterministic: {同结构}}`\n- 复用 death_decomposition.py 的 `load_episodes()` 函数（import 或复制）\n\n### Dry-run\n用现有 prescreening + plan_c 部分数据测试脚本，生成预览图表。\n\n### 服务器\nwestb-13313, account root\nPython: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ],
  "context": "plan-005 是 P0 实验，输出论文 Figure 1 (routing heatmap) 和 failure mode taxonomy。数据格式与 death_decomposition.py 相同。当前有 prescreening (25 tasks × 5 eps) + plan_c 部分数据 (4 tasks done)。"
}

Step 2: Train 1.7B implicit router (condition b)\n   python src/ablation_implicit_1_7b.py train \\\n     --data_path results/plan_004_implicit/implicit_sft.jsonl \\\n     --model_name /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B \\\n     --output_dir results/plan_004_implicit/checkpoints/ \\\n     --gpu_id 1 \\\n     --seed 42\n   ```\n   检查 ablation_implicit_1_7b.py 的 train 子命令参数名（用 --help 确认）。\n\n---\n\n### Part 3: post_collection_analysis.sh\n\n创建 `/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/scripts/post_collection_analysis.sh`：\n```bash\n#!/bin/bash\nset -e\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\ncd /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing\n\necho \"=== Plan-001b Post-Collection Analysis ===\"\necho \"Date: $(date)\"\n\n# 1. Count total episodes per task\necho -e \"\\n--- Episode counts per task ---\"\nfor dir in results/plan_c/*/; do\n  task=$(basename \"$dir\")\n  if [ -f \"$dir/episodes.jsonl\" ]; then\n    count=$(wc -l < \"$dir/episodes.jsonl\")\n    echo \"$task: $count episodes\"\n  else\n    echo \"$task: MISSING episodes.jsonl\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# 2. Check expected 15 tasks present\necho -e \"\\n--- Task completeness check ---\"\nEXPECTED=\"find-animal identify-life-stages-1 lifespan-longest-lived-then-shortest-lived lifespan-longest-lived measure-melting-point-unknown-substance find-non-living-thing freeze chemistry-mix grow-fruit inclined-plane-determine-angle identify-life-stages-2\"\nfor task in $EXPECTED; do\n  if [ -f \"results/plan_c/$task/episodes.jsonl\" ]; then\n    echo \"✓ $task\"\n  else\n    echo \"✗ $task MISSING\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# 3. Also check prescreening + mvp reuse data\necho -e \"\\n--- Reuse data check ---\"\nfor src in results/prescreening results/mvp results/research; do\n  count=$(find \"$src\" -name \"episodes.jsonl\" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\n  echo \"$src: $count task directories\"\ndone\n\n# 4. Total episode count across all sources\necho -e \"\\n--- Total episodes (all sources) ---\"\npython3 -c \"\nimport os, json\nfrom collections import defaultdict\ncounts = defaultdict(int)\nfor src in ['results/prescreening', 'results/mvp', 'results/research', 'results/plan_c']:\n    if not os.path.exists(src): continue\n    for task in os.listdir(src):\n        f = os.path.join(src, task, 'episodes.jsonl')\n        if os.path.isfile(f):\n            with open(f) as fh:\n                n = sum(1 for _ in fh)\n            counts[task] += n\nfor task in sorted(counts):\n    print(f'{task}: {counts[task]} episodes')\nprint(f'\\nTotal tasks: {len(counts)}')\nprint(f'Total episodes: {sum(counts.values())}')\n\"\n\n# 5. Data integrity check\necho -e \"\\n--- Data integrity ---\"\npython3 -c \"\nimport os, json\nerrors = []\nfor src in ['results/plan_c']:\n    if not os.path.exists(src): continue\n    for task in os.listdir(src):\n        f = os.path.join(src, task, 'episodes.jsonl')\n        if not os.path.isfile(f): continue\n        with open(f) as fh:\n            for i, line in enumerate(fh):\n                try:\n                    d = json.loads(line)\n                    assert 'always_internal' in d, 'missing always_internal'\n                    assert 'always_deterministic' in d, 'missing always_deterministic'\n                    assert 'final_score' in d['always_internal'], 'missing internal final_score'\n                    assert 'final_score' in d['always_deterministic'], 'missing det final_score'\n                except Exception as e:\n                    errors.append(f'{task} line {i}: {e}')\nif errors:\n    print(f'ERRORS ({len(errors)}):')\n    for e in errors[:10]:\n        print(f'  {e}')\nelse:\n    print('All episodes valid')\n\"\n\necho -e \"\\n=== Analysis complete ===\"\n```\n\n确保脚本可执行：`chmod +x scripts/*.sh`\n\n---\n\n### 验证\n- Part 1: dry-run 日志确认数据加载成功、episode-level split 生效\n- Part 2: implicit_sft.jsonl 非空且格式正确\n- Part 3: 用当前 plan_c 部分数据运行 post_collection_analysis.sh 验证输出\n\n### 服务器\nwestb-13313, account root\nscripts 目录：/root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/scripts/（如不存在则创建）",

{
  "task_type": "analysis:find-animal-c1-impact",
  "project": "simgate-modular-routing",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：分析 find-animal + find-non-living-thing 对 C1 claim 的影响\n\n### 背景\n两个极端案例已收集到 20 episodes 的数据：\n\n**find-animal (20 eps)**:\n- 5 det-preferred，全部来自 death_asymmetry（internal 死亡 + det 存活）\n- 0 个 score_comparison preference\n- 非死亡 delta = 0（所有 both-alive episode 完全平分）\n- 结论：routing signal 100% 来自死亡规避\n\n**find-non-living-thing (20 eps)**:\n- 0% death rate（双模式均无死亡）\n- 纯 score comparison\n- n=20 后 delta ≈ 0（接近 no-preference）\n- 结论：无死亡时两种 modality 近乎等效\n\n### 你要分析的（纯文本分析，不需要 SSH）\n\n**1. 对 C1 \"death-dominated routing\" claim 的支撑强度**\n- 这两个案例作为互补极端（一个纯死亡信号、一个零死亡零信号），对 C1 的论证力有多强？\n- 能否直接做 case study 放论文？如果放，建议放在哪个 section（Results? Discussion?）\n- 与之前的 aggregate 数据（Δ_full=+38.33, Δ_nd=+0.48, r=0.986）结合，论证结构是什么？\n\n**2. C1 表述升级评估**\n当前 C1: \"death events dominate routing signal (~99%)\"\n是否有理由升级到更强的表述？例如：\n- \"routing signal is exclusively driven by death avoidance\"\n- \"in the absence of death events, routing provides no benefit\"\n- \"deterministic routing = death avoidance, not task performance optimization\"\n评估每种表述的证据支撑度和审稿风险。\n\n**3. 对后续 task 数据的预期**\n- 剩余 ~7 tasks 的数据在收集中，是否预期大多数会落在这两个极端之间？\n- prescreening 数据显示 25/30 tasks 是 death-dominated，5 个 genuine-det——这些 genuine-det 在 20 eps 下是否可能也收敛到 death-dominated？\n- 如果 genuine-det tasks 在 20 eps 下 Δ_nd 仍 > 0，对 C1 表述有什么影响？\n\n### 产出\n- 结构化分析报告（按上述 3 个问题）\n- 建议的 C1 表述（保守/标准/激进三档）\n- 论文 case study 段落草稿（~150 字）\n\n### 上下文\n论文目标：EMNLP 2026 ARR short paper (4 pages)。\n当前 framing (D013): C1 = empirical finding (death avoidance ≈ ~99% routing signal), C2 = counterfactual methodology + diagnostic framework。\naggregate 数据：Δ_full=+38.33, Δ_nd=+0.48, death_fraction=98.75%, r=0.986 (30 tasks, 249 episodes)。\nfailure taxonomy: 所有 death 均由 \"focus on location\" 动作触发，internal 144 次 vs deterministic 37 次。",
  "tool_groups": [
    "knowledge"
  ],
  "context": "EMNLP 2026 ARR short paper, DDL 2026-05-25 (11 days). D013 framing: death avoidance dominates routing signal. 论文需要 strong evidence for C1。find-animal 和 find-non-living-thing 是完美互补的极端案例。"
}

Non-death performance differences are small and statistically insignificant across 30 task types.\\n\\n**激进**：The routing signal is death avoidance — in the absence of catastrophic failure differentials, deterministic and internal computation perform equivalently.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Case Study 段落草稿（~150 words）\\n\\n> Two extreme cases illustrate the death-avoidance mechanism. **find-animal** (20 episodes) exhibits exclusive death-driven routing: all 5 deterministic-preferred episodes arise from death asymmetry (internal agent dies; deterministic survives), while both-alive episodes show zero score difference — routing signal is entirely attributable to catastrophic failure prevention. Conversely, **find-non-living-thing** (20 episodes) has zero death rate under both modalities; with death removed as a differentiator, the two computation modes converge to near-identical performance (Δ ≈ 0, no preference). Together, these cases demonstrate that routing signal activates if and only if death rate differs between modalities: present death asymmetry produces strong deterministic preference; absent death asymmetry produces no preference. This complements the aggregate finding (r=0.986) with a mechanistic account: deterministic computation's advantage is concentrated in preventing the \\\"focus on location\\\" hallucination that triggers catastrophic state transitions.\\n\\n（146 words）\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

在零死亡端（零 Δ）。它们不是 outlier，而是 **确认 aggregate pattern 的极端实例**。\\n\\n3. **可解释性**：相比 30-task aggregate 的统计论证，case study 提供 **mechanistic narrative**——审稿人可以直接理解\\\"为什么\\\"，而不仅仅是\\\"相关性有多高\\\"。\\n\\n### 1.2 论文放置建议\\n\\n**放在 Experiments section 的 death decomposition 分析之后，作为 illustrative case study（~150 words）。** 理由：\\n\\n- Short paper 没有独立 Discussion section 的空间\\n- Case study 的功能是 **具象化** aggregate 统计发现，应紧跟主要结果\\n- 不适合放 Introduction（缺少前置方法论说明）\\n- 不适合作为独立 subsection（short paper 篇幅不允许）\\n\\n建议结构：先报告 aggregate 结果（Δ_full, Δ_nd, r, death_fraction），再用 1 段 case study 具象化。\\n\\n### 1.3 与 aggregate 数据结合的论证结构\\n\\n三层递进：\\n\\n1. **Aggregate statistical**：30 tasks, Δ_full=+38.33 vs Δ_nd=+0.48, r=0.986 (p<0.0001), death_fraction=98.75% → 数量级论证\\n2. **Decomposition**：label 分布 ~40% det-preferred / ~1% internal-preferred / ~59% no-preference, 其中 det-preferred 几乎全来自 death asymmetry → 机制论证\\n3. **Extreme case study**：find-animal (pure death signal) vs find-non-living-thing (zero death, zero signal) → 因果论证\\n\\n这个结构从 correlation → mechanism → causation 递进，在 short paper 中足够完整。\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 2. C1 表述升级评估\\n\\n### 三档表述\\n\\n| 档位 | 表述 | 证据支撑 | 审稿风险 |\\n|------|------|----------|----------|\\n| **保守** | \\\"Death avoidance accounts for the vast majority (~99%) of routing signal\\\" | 完全支撑。aggregate death_fraction=98.75%, r=0.986。留有余地。 | **低**。~99% 是数据直接支持的，\\\"vast majority\\\" 不可攻击。 |\\n| **标准** | \\\"Routing signal is almost exclusively driven by death avoidance; non-death performance differences are negligible\\\" | 强支撑。Δ_nd=+0.48 在 30 tasks 上不显著，find-non-living-thing 确认无死亡时 delta→0。 | **中低**。\\\"almost exclusively\\\" 准确但激进。审稿人可能要求 significance test on Δ_nd。 |\\n| **激进** | \\\"In the absence of death events, routing provides no benefit — deterministic routing equals death avoidance\\\" | 部分支撑。find-non-living-thing 是直接证据，但仅 1 个 zero-death task。Δ_nd=+0.48 > 0 虽小但非严格零。 | **高**。\\\"no benefit\\\" 是全称命题，1 个 task 不能证明。若 genuine-det tasks 在 20 eps 下 Δ_nd > 0，此表述直接被推翻。\\\"equals\\\" 过强。 |\\n\\n### 建议\\n\\n**采用标准档**，措辞微调为：\\n\\n> \\\"Death avoidance accounts for ~99% of the routing signal between internal and deterministic computation (Δ_full=+38.33 vs Δ_nd=+0.48, r=0.986). Non-death score differences are small and inconsistent across task types.\\\"\\n\\n理由：\\n- 保守档太弱——你有 r=0.986 和两个极端 case study，不需要这么谦虚\\n- 激进档的 \\\"no benefit\\\" 和 \\\"equals\\\" 是全称命题，5 个 genuine-det tasks 的 20-ep 数据可能产生反例（见第 3 节）\\n- 标准档既利用了极端 case study 的论证力，又用 \\\"almost exclusively\\\" 和 \\\"negligible\\\" 留了 buffer\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 3. 对后续 task 数据的预期\\n\\n### 3.1 剩余 ~7 tasks 是否落在两极之间？\\n\\n**大概率是，但分布会偏向 death-dominated 端。**\\n\\nPrescreening 25/30 tasks 是 death-dominated。剩余 7 tasks 中包含 5 genuine-det + 2 death-dom（来自 report.md 的 plan-001b-gpu0-v2）。death-dom 的 2 个会确认 pattern。5 个 genuine-det 是关键。\\n\\n### 3.2 Genuine-det 在 20 eps 下是否收敛到 death-dominated？\\n\\n**有较高概率。** 证据：\\n\\n- D012 已指出：prescreening 中 find-living-thing (+0.25) 和 freeze (+2.0) 在 n=5 下无统计意义，仅 find-animal (+4.2) 可能有真实信号\\n- D016 记录了 find-non-living-thing 从 -6.8 收窄到 -2.3 再到 ≈0 的轨迹——说明 n=5 的 genuine-det/internal-pref 分类噪声大\\n- find-animal 在 20 eps 下确认 non-death delta = 0（both-alive 时完全平分），说明 n=5 时的 +4.2 delta 也来自死亡，不是 genuine score advantage\\n\\n**预期**：5 个 genuine-det 中 3-4 个会在 20 eps 下收敛为 death-dominated（Δ_nd→0），1-2 个可能保留小的正 Δ_nd（<2pp，不显著）。\\n\\n### 3.3 若 genuine-det 在 20 eps 下 Δ_nd 仍 > 0\\n\\n**对 C1 标准档表述影响很小，但阻止激进档。**\\n\\n- 如果 Δ_nd 在 1-3pp 范围且不显著（bootstrap CI 包含 0）：C1 不受影响，\\\"negligible\\\" 仍成立。可以在论文中报告为 \\\"small, not statistically significant\\\"\\n- 如果某个 task 的 Δ_nd > 5pp 且显著：C1 需要从 \\\"~99%\\\" 降级到 \\\"~95%\\\" 或 \\\"dominant majority\\\"。但这反而增加了论文的 nuance，不是坏事——\\\"death avoidance dominates but genuine performance advantages exist in specific task categories\\\" 是更有说服力的表述\\n- 即使出现后者，aggregate r=0.986 和 death_fraction 不会大幅变化（单个 task 的权重有限），核心 claim 安全\\n\\n---\\n\\n## C1 建议表述（三档最终版）\\n\\n**保守**：Death avoidance accounts for the vast majority of routing signal between internal LLM reasoning and deterministic computation on ScienceWorld.\\n\\n**标准（推荐）**：Death avoidance accounts for ~99% of the routing signal (Δ_full=+38.33 vs Δ_nd=+0.48, r=0.986, p<0.0001). Non-death performance differences are small and statistically insignificant across 30 task types.\\n\\n**激进**：The routing signal is death avoidance — in the absence of catastrophic failure differentials, deterministic and internal computation perform equivalently.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## Case Study 段落草稿（~150 words）\\n\\n> Two extreme cases illustrate the death-avoidance mechanism. **find-animal** (20 episodes) exhibits exclusive death-driven routing: all 5 deterministic-preferred episodes arise from death asymmetry (internal agent dies; deterministic survives), while both-alive episodes show zero score difference — routing signal is entirely attributable to catastrophic failure prevention. Conversely, **find-non-living-thing** (20 episodes) has zero death rate under both modalities; with death removed as a differentiator, the two computation modes converge to near-identical performance (Δ ≈ 0, no preference). Together, these cases demonstrate that routing signal activates if and only if death rate differs between modalities: present death asymmetry produces strong deterministic preference; absent death asymmetry produces no preference. This complements the aggregate finding (r=0.986) with a mechanistic account: deterministic computation's advantage is concentrated in preventing the \\\"focus on location\\\" hallucination that triggers catastrophic state transitions.\\n\\n（146 words）\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"
    }
  },

_source line 636 · 2026-05-14T00:31:06.269Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   This is an ML research agent session for the **simgate-modular-routing** project, targeting **EMNLP 2026 ARR short paper** (DDL 2026-05-25, 11 days remaining). The core research finding is that **death avoidance dominates ~99% of routing signal** in LLM agent routing on ScienceWorld. The session involved:
   - Processing multiple worker completions and cron callbacks
   - Executing Director instructions for experiment management
   - Managing plan-001b episode-level data collection on dual GPUs (westb-13313, 2× RTX 5090 32GB)
   - Preparing downstream analysis and training code (plan-002, plan-004, plan-005, plan-012)
   - Diagnosing and fixing GPU crashes (vLLM orphan process issue)
   - Analyzing experimental results for paper claims (C1 death-dominated routing)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Death avoidance routing**: Core finding — routing signal between internal LLM reasoning and deterministic computation is ~99% attributable to death avoidance (Δ_full=+38.33, Δ_nd=+0.48, r=0.986)
   - **Episode-level counterfactual labeling**: Running always-internal vs always-deterministic episodes, labeling by episode total score (three labels: det-preferred, internal-preferred, no-preference)
   - **vLLM**: Used for LLM inference (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct), runs as EngineCore subprocess
   - **ScienceWorld**: Java-based environment (py4j), fork-and-evaluate approach causes burst GPU utilization pattern
   - **Death decomposition**: Analyzing Δ_full (all episodes) vs Δ_nd (non-death episodes), death_avoidance_fraction (DAF), correlation between death rate and Δ(D-I)
   - **Router SFT**: Fine-tuning Qwen3-1.7B as 2-way classifier (internal/deterministic), episode-level train/val split to prevent data leakage
   - **Ablation framework**: Three conditions — (a) 1.7B explicit router, (b) 1.7B implicit [SIMULATE] token, (c) 7B tool-use prompt
   - **Keyword heuristic structural failure**: v1 (always-det) and v2 (always-internal) both fail, positioned as positive finding

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`src/episode_level_collection.py`** (server: /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/)
     - Core data collection script for plan-001b
     - Modified: Added `cleanup_gpu_processes()` function (lines 36-60) to auto-kill residual vLLM processes on target GPU before agent creation — fixes recurring GPU0 OOM crash
     - Modified: `label_episode()` → `classify_episode_labels()` three-label scheme
     - Modified: `run_task_collection()` now outputs `episodes.jsonl` format
     - Supports `--reuse_dirs` for skipping already-collected episodes
   
   - **`src/death_decomposition.py`** (519 lines)
     - CORE analysis script for plan-012
     - Supports auto-scan of data directories via `--data_dirs` (comma-separated)
     - Loads episodes via `load_episodes()` with `os.listdir()` scanning for `episodes.jsonl`
     - Auto-merges same task from multiple directories using `defaultdict(list)`
     - Run command: `python src/death_decomposition.py --data_dirs results/prescreening,results/mvp,results/research,results/plan_c --output results/decomposition_final/death_decomposition_report.json`
   
   - **`src/plot_death_decomposition.py`**
     - Generates paper figures: scatter plot (death_rate_diff vs Δ), label distribution bar chart, DAF bar chart
     - Outputs PDF (paper) + PNG (preview)
   
   - **`src/router_sft_train.py`** (506 lines)
     - Router SFT training for plan-002
     - Fixed: `stratified_split()` changed to episode-level split using `(task_type, episode_id, variation)` grouping
     - Fixed: `metric_for_best_model` from `"accuracy"` to `"f1_macro"`
     - Added `f1_macro` and `balanced_accuracy` to `compute_metrics()`
     - Supports `--data_path` for new sft_triples.jsonl format
     - Labels: `internal-preferred` → `internal`, `det-preferred` → `deterministic`, `no-preference` filtered out
     - Prompt: Qwen3 ChatML format
   
   - **`src/death_aware_labeling.py`**
     - Generates sft_triples.jsonl from episodes.jsonl
     - Three-label classification: det-preferred, internal-preferred, no-preference
     - Output format: `{context, int_action, det_action, label, signal_source, task_type, episode_idx}`
   
   - **`src/ablation_eval_common.py`** (327 lines)
     - Shared evaluation framework for plan-004
     - Defines `RouterFn` protocol: `(obs, valid_actions, history) → (action, route)`
     - Core functions: `load_oracle_labels`, `run_routed_episode`, `compute_routing_accuracy`, `run_ablation_eval`
   
   - **`src/ablation_data_converter.py`** (146 lines)
     - Converts sft_triples.jsonl → implicit_sft.jsonl (chat format with [SIMULATE] prefix)
     - CLI: `--input`, `--output` (actual param names: `--input_path`, `--output_path`)
   
   - **`src/ablation_implicit_1_7b.py`** (348 lines)
     - Condition (b): Qwen3-1.7B causal LM + LoRA SFT
     - `ImplicitSFTDataset` for chat template tokenization
     - `parse_implicit_output` handles [SIMULATE] prefix
     - CLI subcommands: `{train, eval}`
   
   - **`src/ablation_implicit_7b.py`** (223 lines)
     - Condition (c): Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct tool-use prompt (no training)
     - `Implicit7BRouter` with vLLM/HF dual backend
     - `parse_tool_use_output` + `_snap_to_valid` fuzzy matching
   
   - **`src/routing_pattern_analysis.py`**
     - Plan-005: per-task routing pattern extraction, heatmap figure, failure mode taxonomy
     - Found all deaths from "focus on &lt;location>" action (internal 144x vs deterministic 37x)
   
   - **`src/rule_based_keywords_v2.json`** — 17 science verbs (plan-011, closed)
   - **`src/rule_based_accuracy.py`** — defaults to v2, compatible with v1/v2
   - **`src/postprocess_to_sft.py`** — DELETED (146 lines, outdated)
   
   - **`scripts/launch_plan002.sh`** — plan-002 router SFT launch (cuda:0)
   - **`scripts/launch_plan004b.sh`** — plan-004 condition (b) launch (cuda:1)
   - **`scripts/post_collection_analysis.sh`** — post-collection data completeness check
   
   - **`artifacts/plan_001b_revised_proposal.md`** — Final plan-001b proposal (15 tasks, 185 new eps, ~7h dual GPU)
   - **`artifacts/prescreening_final_summary.json`** — 25/25 task classification
   
   - **Model**: Qwen3-1.7B downloaded at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B` (3.8GB, vocab_size=151643)

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **GPU0 OOM Crash (recurring, 2 times)**:
     - Root cause: vLLM EngineCore child processes become orphans when parent script crashes, holding ~28 GiB GPU memory. No cleanup mechanism existed.
     - First fix attempt: Killed residual process (pid 741465), resubmitted. But crashed again because same pattern repeated.
     - Final fix: Added `cleanup_gpu_processes()` function to `episode_level_collection.py` (lines 36-60) that auto-kills residual processes on target GPU before agent creation. Resubmitted as plan-001b-gpu0-v2 (PID 853847).
     - Director feedback: "不是简单的'残留进程占显存'，上次杀了残留重提交后又在 init 阶段崩溃。诊断步骤..."
   
   - **Registry state transition error (PREPARING → RUNNING)**:
     - Error: "Invalid transition: PREPARING → RUNNING. Allowed: ['PENDING', 'QUEUED']"
     - Fix: Went through intermediate states: PREPARING → QUEUED → RUNNING
   
   - **Artifact registration error (content type)**:
     - Error: "Input should be a valid string" when passing dict as content
     - Fix: Changed content from JSON dict to plain text string
   
   - **GPU1 speed anomaly report**:
     - Director reported 9.5h for 2 episodes (70x slower than expected)
     - Diagnosis: Speed was actually normal (~4.7 min/episode). Report was based on outdated info.
     - Deterministic mode inherently slow by design: 30 steps × 3 JVM forks = 90 JVM restarts per episode
   
   - **decomposition_report.json overwritten**:
     - Root-level file overwritten with partial 2-task data
     - Fix: Copied complete 30-task version from decomposition_dryrun/ directory
   
   - **Data leakage in router_sft_train.py**:
     - Reviewer found stratified_split() doing step-level random split — same episode's ~30 steps share label and correlated observations
     - Fix: Changed to episode-level split using (task_type, episode_id, variation) grouping
   
   - **Best model metric meaningless**:
     - accuracy metric useless with 102 det vs 1 internal (always-det = 99%)
     - Fix: Changed to f1_macro
   
   - **Orphan prescreening processes**:
     - PID 823770 (signal_prescreening.py) and PID 649683 (run_prescreening_gpu0.sh) still running despite registry marked killed

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user is the AI execution layer (Main Agent) for the simgate-modular-routing ML research project, targeting EMNLP 2026 ARR short paper (DDL 2026-05-25, 11 days remaining). The core research finding is that death avoidance dominates ~99% of LLM agent routing signal in ScienceWorld. The session involved: processing worker callbacks, executing Director instructions, managing dual-GPU data collection (plan-001b), debugging GPU crashes, preparing downstream experiment code (plan-002 router SFT, plan-004 ablation, plan-005 routing patterns, plan-012 death decomposition), and analyzing results for the paper's C1 claim. The most recent Director instruction requests 5 parallel tasks while gpu0 finishes plan-001b: (1) release gpu1 vLLM, (2) plan-012 pre-analysis, (3) plan-002 code prep, (4) plan-011 rule-based baseline code, (5) gpu1 data integrity verification.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Death avoidance dominance**: Δ_full=+38.33 vs Δ_nd=+0.48, r=0.986, death_fraction=98.75% across 30 tasks
   - **Episode-level counterfactual labeling**: Plan C approach — run complete always-internal + always-deterministic episodes, label by episode total score
   - **Three-label scheme**: det-preferred / internal-preferred / no-preference + signal_source field
   - **vLLM EngineCore orphan process problem**: Child processes survive parent crash, hold GPU memory. Fix: cleanup_gpu_processes() auto-kills before init
   - **ScienceWorld fork-and-evaluate**: Each deterministic episode step does 3× JVM fork + replay full action history. ~200s/episode deterministic vs ~31s internal
   - **Episode-level data split**: Fixed data leakage where same-episode steps leaked across train/val. Now splits by (task_type, episode_idx, variation)
   - **Router SFT**: Qwen3-1.7B binary classification (internal/deterministic), f1_macro metric due to extreme class imbalance
   - **Ablation framework**: 3 conditions — (a) 1.7B explicit router, (b) 1.7B implicit [SIMULATE] token, (c) 7B tool-use prompt
   - **Failure taxonomy**: ALL deaths from `focus on &lt;location>` hallucination action, internal 144x vs deterministic 37x

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`src/episode_level_collection.py`** (server: /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/)
     - Critical fix: Added `cleanup_gpu_processes()` (lines 36-60) to kill orphan vLLM EngineCore processes before model init
     - Pipeline format fix: `label_episode()` → `classify_episode_labels()` 3-label scheme
     - New `episodes.jsonl` output from `run_task_collection()`
     - `scan_reuse_episodes()` for --reuse_dirs support
   
   - **`src/router_sft_train.py`** (506 lines, server)
     - Reviewer-mandated fixes applied: episode-level stratified split, f1_macro metric, balanced_accuracy added
     - Data loading: `--data_path` for sft_triples.jsonl, filters no-preference labels
     - Prompt template: Qwen3 ChatML format
     - Hyperparams: lr=2e-5, batch=32, epochs=3, max_seq_len=2048, class_weights auto
   
   - **`src/death_decomposition.py`** (519 lines, server)
     - Auto-scans --data_dirs for episodes.jsonl subdirectories, merges same-task data
     - 6 analyses: Δ_full, Δ_nd, death_fraction, correlation, penalty-removed, label distribution
     - Outputs: JSON report + paper_table1_data.json + 3 figures (PDF+PNG)
   
   - **`src/routing_pattern_analysis.py`** (server)
     - Per-task routing metrics, routing heatmap figure, failure mode taxonomy
     - Found: all deaths from `focus on &lt;location>`, most deadly: `focus on workshop`
   
   - **`src/ablation_eval_common.py`** (327 lines) — RouterFn protocol, run_routed_episode(), shared eval
   - **`src/ablation_data_converter.py`** (146 lines) — sft_triples.jsonl → implicit_sft.jsonl with [SIMULATE] prefix
   - **`src/ablation_implicit_1_7b.py`** (348 lines) — Condition (b): Qwen3-1.7B LoRA SFT, [SIMULATE] token parsing
   - **`src/ablation_implicit_7b.py`** (223 lines) — Condition (c): Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct tool-use prompt eval
   
   - **`src/death_aware_labeling.py`** (server) — Generates sft_triples.jsonl from episodes.jsonl, classify_episode() 3-label
   - **`src/rule_based_keywords_v2.json`** (server) — 17 science verbs, proven always-internal
   - **`src/rule_based_accuracy.py`** (server) — Supports v1/v2 keyword format
   - **`src/postprocess_to_sft.py`** — DELETED (obsolete, hardcoded old paths)
   
   - **`scripts/launch_plan002.sh`** — Full plan-002 launch: labeling → router SFT training (cuda:0)
   - **`scripts/launch_plan004b.sh`** — Full plan-004b launch: data convert → implicit 1.7B train (cuda:1)
   - **`scripts/post_collection_analysis.sh`** — Post-collection validation: episode counts, completeness, integrity
   
   - **`artifacts/plan_001b_revised_proposal.md`** — Final plan-001b proposal (15 tasks, 185 new eps, ~7h dual GPU)
   - **`artifacts/prescreening_final_summary.json`** — 25/25 tasks classification
   - **`memory.md`** — Decision records and experience lessons
   - **Model**: Qwen3-1.7B downloaded at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B` (3.8GB, vocab_size=151643)

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **GPU0 vLLM OOM crash (×2)**: vLLM EngineCore child processes became orphans when parent script crashed, holding ~28GB GPU memory. Each resubmission hit OOM because orphan still held memory.
     - Fix: Added `cleanup_gpu_processes()` function to episode_level_collection.py (lines 36-60) that auto-kills residual processes on target GPU before agent creation
     - Director confirmed fix, noted this was not a simple "residual process" issue but a systematic design flaw
   
   - **Data leakage in router_sft_train.py**: stratified_split() was splitting at step-level, but same-episode steps share labels and observations
     - Fix: Changed to episode-level split by grouping on (task_type, episode_id, variation)
     - Reviewer-mandated fix
   
   - **Best model metric meaningless**: accuracy=99% with always-det on 102:1 imbalance
     - Fix: Changed metric_for_best_model from "accuracy" to "f1_macro"

     - Fix: kill 823770 649683, confirmed no residuals
   
   - **Artifact registration type error**: content parameter passed as dict instead of string
     - Fix: Passed content as plain text string instead of JSON object
   
   - **Registry state transition error**: PREPARING → RUNNING not allowed directly
     - Fix: Transition through PREPARING → QUEUED → RUNNING with spec

5. Problem Solving:
   - **GPU0 crash root cause**: Identified systematic vLLM EngineCore orphan process pattern. Added code-level fix (cleanup_gpu_processes()) rather than manual process killing.
   - **GPU1 speed anomaly**: Diagnosed as normal behavior — ScienceWorld Java fork-and-evaluate pattern causes burst GPU utilization. ~4.7 min/episode is expected.
   - **Plan-001b stall check**: Confirmed via 3-minute episode snapshot comparison (96→99 episodes). GPU 0% utilization during Java interaction phases is normal.
   - **Keyword heuristic structural failure**: v1=always-det, v2=always-internal. Positioned as positive finding for paper (structural failure analysis).
   - **C1 claim strength**: Analyzed find-animal (100% death signal) + find-non-living-thing (0% death, 0 signal) as complementary extreme cases. Recommended standard-tier C1 wording with ~99% claim.

|\n|-------|------|---------|\n| det-preferred | int died + det alive，或 both alive det > int | ~38% |\n| internal-preferred | det died + int alive，或 both alive int > det | ~1-2% |\n| no-preference | both died，或 both alive tie | ~60% |\n\n**Class imbalance 应对**: SFT 训练用 class-weighted loss（internal 样本权重 ×5）+ find-non-living-thing 扩大到 40 eps。\n\n---\n\n## 5. 最终 Task Selection（方案 A 确认）\n\n**15 tasks = 5 genuine-det + 1 internal-pref + 5 death-dom (新采) + 4 MVP (复用)**\n\n| # | Task | Source | Signal | Existing eps | New eps needed | Target |\n|---|------|--------|--------|-------------:|---------------:|-------:|\n| 1 | find-animal | prescreen | genuine-det | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 2 | identify-life-stages-1 | prescreen | genuine-det | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 3 | lifespan-longest-lived-then-shortest | prescreen | genuine-det | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 4 | lifespan-longest-lived | prescreen | genuine-det | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 5 | measure-melting-point-unknown | prescreen | genuine-det | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 6 | **find-non-living-thing** | prescreen | **int-pref** | 5 | **35** | **40** |\n| 7 | freeze | prescreen | death-dom | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 8 | chemistry-mix | prescreen | death-dom | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 9 | grow-fruit | prescreen | death-dom | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 10 | inclined-plane-determine-angle | prescreen | death-dom | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 11 | identify-life-stages-2 | prescreen | death-dom | 5 | 15 | 20 |\n| 12 | boil | MVP | death-dom | **20** | **0** | 20 |\n| 13 | melt | MVP | death-dom | **20** | **0** | 20 |\n| 14 | change-the-state-of-matter-of | MVP | death-dom | **20** | **0** | 20 |\n| 15 | chemistry-mix-paint-secondary-color | MVP | death-dom | **20** | **0** | 20 |\n\n**排除**: grow-plant (no-effect), 其余 14 个 death-dominated prescreening tasks（已有足够 death-dom 样本）\n\n| 合计 | 值 |\n|------|---|\n| 总 tasks | 15 |\n| 现有 episodes | 135 (5×11 prescreen + 4×20 MVP) |\n| 需新增 episodes | 185 (15×11 prescreen + 35 find-non-living extra) |\n| 最终总 episodes | 320 |\n| Signal 覆盖 | death-dom:9, genuine-det:5, int-pref:1 |\n\n### find-non-living-thing 40 eps 方案可行性\n\n- 0% death rate（双模式），所有 episode 都产生有效 training signal\n- max_variations=300，40 episodes 仅消耗 13% variation 空间，无重复风险\n- 预估 40 eps 产出 ~40 个 internal-preferred labels（占总训练集 ~12%）\n- 配合 class-weighted loss，足以让 router 学到 internal-preferred 模式\n\n---\n\n## 6. GPU 时间估算\n\n### 基准\n\n从预筛日志推算：5 episodes × 2 modes ≈ 20 min → **4 min/episode-pair**\n\n### 时间表\n\n| 组别 | Tasks | New eps | Time (单卡) |\n|------|------:|--------:|------------:|\n| genuine-det (5 tasks × 15 eps) | 5 | 75 | 300 min |\n| int-pref (find-non-living, 35 new eps) | 1 | 35 | 140 min |\n| death-dom (5 tasks × 15 eps) | 5 | 75 | 300 min |\n| MVP (reuse, 0 new) | 4 | 0 | 0 min |\n| **合计** | **15** | **185** | **740 min ≈ 12.3h** |\n\n### GPU 分配策略（双卡并行）\n\n| GPU | 任务分配 | 预计时间 |\n|-----|---------|---------|\n| cuda:0 | genuine-det 5 tasks (75 eps) + death-dom 2 tasks (30 eps) = 105 eps | ~7h |\n| cuda:1 | find-non-living-thing 35 eps + death-dom 3 tasks (45 eps) = 80 eps | ~5.3h |\n\n**总挂钟时间**: ~7h（含启动/模型加载开销 ~30min）\n\n---\n\n## 7. 数据格式与复用\n\n### 复用验证 ✅\n\nPrescreening `episodes.jsonl` 与 `episode_level_collection.py` 输出格式一致（pipeline-format-fix 已确认）：\n- 均存储 `always_internal` + `always_deterministic` paired episodes\n- 含 step-level: `step`, `action`, `modality`, `score_before`, `score_after`\n- `death_aware_labeling.py` 的 `classify_episode()` 可直接消费\n- 三标签输出: `det-preferred` / `internal-preferred` / `no-preference` + `signal_source`\n\n### 复用路径\n\n```\n--reuse_dirs results/prescreening,results/mvp,results/research\n```\n\n`scan_reuse_episodes()` 自动扫描已完成 episodes，`effective_start` 从已有数量续跑。\n\n---\n\n## 8. 提交命令\n\n### GPU0\n```bash\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\ncd /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing\n\nnohup python src/episode_level_collection.py \\\n  --task_types \"find-animal,identify-life-stages-1,lifespan-longest-lived-then-shortest-lived,lifespan-longest-lived,measure-melting-point-unknown-substance,freeze,chemistry-mix\" \\\n  --episodes 20 \\\n  --gpu_id 0 \\\n  --output_dir results/plan_c/ \\\n  --reuse_dirs results/prescreening,results/mvp,results/research \\\n  --seed 42 \\\n  > results/plan_c/gpu0_run.log 2>&1 &\n```\n\n### GPU1\n```bash\nnohup python src/episode_level_collection.py \\\n  --task_types \"find-non-living-thing,grow-fruit,inclined-plane-determine-angle,identify-life-stages-2\" \\\n  --episodes 20 \\\n  --gpu_id 1 \\\n  --output_dir results/plan_c/ \\\n  --reuse_dirs results/prescreening,results/mvp,results/research \\\n  --seed 42 \\\n  > results/plan_c/gpu1_run.log 2>&1 &\n```\n\n**find-non-living-thing 追加到 40 eps**:\n```bash\nnohup python src/episode_level_collection.py \\\n  --task_types \"find-non-living-thing\" \\\n  --episodes 40 \\\n  --start_episode 20 \\\n  --gpu_id 1 \\\n  --output_dir results/plan_c/ \\\n  --seed 43 \\\n  > results/plan_c/gpu1_fnlt_extra.log 2>&1 &\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9. 预期产出\n\n| 下游 Plan | 需要的数据 | 本次提供 |\n|-----------|-----------|---------|\n| plan-012 (death decomposition) | 15+ tasks × 20 eps 的 death rate + score 数据 | ✅ 15 tasks, 320 episodes |\n| plan-005 (routing heatmap) | 多 task cross-domain episode 数据 | ✅ 5 domains covered |\n| plan-002 (router SFT) | labeled step triples (context, int_action, det_action, label) | ✅ ~3200 steps |\n| plan-011 (rule-based baseline) | task-level death keywords + episode outcomes | ✅ same data |\n\n### 预期 label 分布（基于预筛 extrapolation）\n\n| Label | 估算数量 | 占比 |\n|-------|---------|-----|\n| det-preferred | ~120 | ~38% |\n| internal-preferred | ~15 (含 find-non-living ~40) | ~5% (upsampled → ~15%) |\n| no-preference (discard/tie) | ~185 | ~58% |\n\n---\n\n## 10. 风险与缓解\n\n| 风险 | 概率 | 影响 | 缓解 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| genuine-det 在 20 eps 下不再显著 | 30% | 高 | 5 个 task 中只需 2-3 个存活即可 |\n| internal labels 仍 < 5% | 60% | 中 | class-weighted loss + find-non-living 40 eps |\n| vLLM OOM on 双卡并行 | 10% | 低 | 已验证 prescreening 双卡稳定运行 |\n| 某些 task reuse 格式不兼容 | 5% | 低 | pipeline-format-fix 已验证兼容 |\n\n---\n\n## 11. Timeline\n\n```\nT+0h          预筛全部完成 ✅，pipeline-format-fix ✅，方案确认\nT+0.5h        启动 plan_c 双卡并行\nT+7h          数据采集完成\nT+8h          运行 death_aware_labeling.py → labeled data\nT+9h          统计 label 分布，确认是否满足 plan-002 需求\nT+9h          → plan-012 death decomposition 分析\nT+10h         → plan-002 router SFT 启动\n```\n\n**距 DDL (2026-05-25) 还剩 11 天，plan-001b 占 ~0.5 天。**\n",
